        exec: function(permission, tool, owner) {
            var crud = permission.charAt(0);
            var roles = [];
            if ($can.permissions.hasOwnProperty(tool) && $can.permissions[tool].hasOwnProperty(crud)) {
                roles = $can.permissions[tool][crud];
            }
            if (roles.indexOf('*') > -1) {
                return !$rgu.is('banned');
            }
            if (roles.indexOf('approved') > -1) {
                return !$rgu.is('banned') && ($rgu.is('unapproved') && $rgu.postCount < 1 || !$rgu.is('unapproved'));
            }
            if (roles.indexOf('not_banned') > -1 && $rgu.is('banned')) {
                return false;
            }
            if (crud != 'r' && ($rgu.is('unapproved') || $rgu.is('unverified'))) {
                return false;
            }
            if (roles.indexOf('owner') > -1 && owner) {
                return true;
            }
            for (index in roles) {
                if ($rgu.is(roles[index])) {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            return false;
        },

<script>
    $(document).ready(function() {
        $console = $('div.content-schedule-console');
        $ContentSchedule.init($console.data('limit'), $console.data('update-url'), $console.data('sport'));
        
        text_truncate = function(str, length) {
            if (str.length> length) {
                return str.substring(0, length);
            }
        }
        
        const sportSelect = document.querySelector('.sport-dropdown');
        $(sportSelect).find('option').each(function(){
            let text = $(this).text();
            if (text.length > 3) {
                text = text.substring(0, 3);
                $(this).text(text);
            }
        });
        
        const days = [];
        $('.lst.week').children('li').each(function(){
            $(this).children('a').text(function(){
                days.push($(this).text())
            });
        });
        
        const delay = 100;  // Your delay here

        const originalResize = evt => {
            if (window.innerWidth < 1010) {
                $('.lst.week').children('li').each(function(){
                    $(this).children('a').text(function(){
                        if ($(this).text() == 'Today') {
                            return 'Today';
                        }
                        return text_truncate($(this).text(), 3);
                    });
                });
            }
            else {
                $('.lst.week').children('li').each(function(i){
                    $(this).children('a').text(function(){
                        return days[i];
                    });
                });
            }
        };
        
        (() => {
          resizeTaskId = null;
        
          window.addEventListener('resize', evt => {
            if (resizeTaskId !== null) {
              clearTimeout(resizeTaskId);
            }
        
            resizeTaskId = setTimeout(() => {
              resizeTaskId = null;
              originalResize(evt);
            }, delay);
          });
        })();
        
        document.querySelector('.sport-dropdown').addEventListener('click', originalResize());
        originalResize();
        
    });
</script>

        $(document).ready(function() {

            // ResultsDB Slider
            $.get('https://resultsdb-api.rotogrinders.com/resultsdb-api/api/contests-summary', function(data) {
                // Hide the trophy icon since we want to see the chart
                if (data && data.rgWinnerContests.length) {
                    $('.resultsTrophy').hide();
                    var $slider = $('[data-role="horz-slider4"]');
                    var listItems = [];
                    var totalPrizes = data.totalPrizes;
                    var totalRgPrizes = data.rgPrizes;
                    if (totalPrizes > 0 && totalRgPrizes > 0) {
                        var tournamentText = 1 == data.contestCount ? 'tournament' : 'tournaments';
                        var summary = "<p>Yesterday, RotoGrinders members won $" + totalRgPrizes.toFixed().replace(/(\d)(?=(\d{3})+(,|$))/g, '$1,') + " on DraftKings.                         $slider.prepend('<li>' + summary + '</li>');
                    }
                    $.each(data.rgWinnerContests, function(index, value) {
                        $slider.append('<li><p>Congrats to <a href="/profiles/' + value.user.rgUserName + '">' + value.user.rgUserName + '</a> for winning $' + value.prize.cash.toFixed().replace(/(\d)(?=(\d{3})+(,|$))/g, '$1,') + ' in the <a href="' + value.contestUrl + '">' + value.contestName + '</a> on ' + value.contestDay + '!</p></li>')
                    });
                    $slider.slick({
                        "slide": "li",
                        "slidesToShow": 1,
                        "slidesToScroll": 1,
                        "autoplay": true,
                    });
                    var pieData = [
                    { value: totalPrizes - totalRgPrizes },
                    { value: totalRgPrizes },
                ];
                
                var total = totalPrizes;
                
                var width = 78;
                var height = 78;
                var radius = Math.min(width, height) / 2;
                
                var color = ['#0094d9', '#f98c13'];
                var svg = d3
                    .select('#chart')
                    .append('svg')
                    .attr('width', width)
                    .attr('height', height)
                    .append('g')
                    .attr('transform', 'translate(' + (width / 2) + ',' + (height / 2) + ')');
            
                var arc = d3
                    .arc()
                    .innerRadius(0)
                    .outerRadius(radius);
            
                var pie = d3.pie()
                    .value(function(d) { return d.value; })
                    .sort(null);
            
                var path = svg.selectAll('path')
                    .data(pie(pieData))
                    .enter()
                    .append('path')
                    .attr('d', arc)
                    .attr('fill', function(d, i) {
                        return color[i];
                    });
                $('#chart').show();
                $slider.on('beforeChange', function(event, slick, currentSlide, nextSlide){
                    if (nextSlide === 0) {
                        $('.resultsTrophy').fadeOut('slow', function() {
                            $('#chart').fadeIn();
                        });
                    } else {
                        $('#chart').fadeOut('slow', function() {
                            $('.resultsTrophy').fadeIn();
                        });
                    }
                });
                } else {
                    $('#congrats').hide();
                }
            });
